*   12 - -7 = 12 + 7 = 19

*   2 - 4 = -2

**Step 2: Evaluate the second set of parentheses (-5 - 6 - 5 - -4)**

**Step 2: Evaluate the second set of inner parentheses.**

First, perform the multiplication: -5 * -8.
-5 * -8 = 40 (A negative number multiplied by a negative number yields a positive number).
Now, substitute this back into the expression:
-3 + 5 - 40
Perform the addition and subtraction from left to right:
-3 + 5 = 2
2 - 40 = -38
So, the value of the second part is -38.

**Step 3: Sum the results from Step 1 and Step 2.**
2 + (-38)
2 - 38 = -36

Therefore, the final answer is -36.

Final Answer: -36
